# ​D14.2 The PMU event number space and common events

Source: <https://developer.arm.com/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D14-PMU-Event-Descriptions/-D14-2-The-PMU-event-number-space-and-common-events>

#### D14.2 The PMU event number space and common events

In Armv8.0, the event number space is 10 bits. Armv8.1 extends the event number space, and therefore the [PMEVTYPER<n>](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-K-Appendixes/-Appendix-K14-Registers-Index/-K14-1-Introduction-and-register-disambiguation/-K14-1-1-Register-name-disambiguation-by-Execution-state?lang=en#cihhcaec).evtCount field, to 16 bits, and is allocated as [Table D14-2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D14-PMU-Event-Descriptions/-D14-2-The-PMU-event-number-space-and-common-events?lang=en#tbl_cacbhbfj) shows.

For more information about the entries in the [Allocation](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D14-PMU-Event-Descriptions/-D14-2-The-PMU-event-number-space-and-common-events?lang=en#cacddaif) column see the text that follows this table:

<table id="tbl_cacbhbfj">
 <caption>
  Table D14-2 Allocation of the PMU event number space
 </caption>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
   </th>
   <th>
    Event numbers
   </th>
   <th>
    <span id="cacddaif">
     Allocation
    </span>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>

  <tr>

    -

     </a>

See [PMEVTYPER<n>](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-K-Appendixes/-Appendix-K14-Registers-Index/-K14-1-Introduction-and-register-disambiguation/-K14-1-1-Register-name-disambiguation-by-Execution-state?lang=en#cihhcaec).evtCount for details of the PE behavior when an event number for a reserved or unimplemented PMU event is written to evtCount.
